- Source: final rulings, boss-E33 (`06_final_rulings.md`); Last Giant booklet lines 452-462

### CH-21-037 · Glossary, Shaft
- Type: clarification
- Before: "**Shaft.** A weapon icon. An attack option with the shaft icon cannot target a model on the attacker's own node (see §10.3, Shaft)."
- After: "**Shaft.** A weapon icon. An attack option with the shaft icon cannot target a model on the attacker's own node (see §10.3, Shaft). On a behaviour card of Manus, Father of the Abyss, an attack with the shaft icon does not target a character on Manus's own node (see §17.10, Manus, Father of the Abyss)."
- Rationale: The icon also appears on a boss's cards.
- Source: final rulings, boss-E34 (`06_final_rulings.md`); Manus booklet lines 446-466

### CH-21-038 · Glossary, Aggro token
- Type: ruling
- Before: "**Aggro token.** The single token that marks the character with aggro. It is placed on the character with the highest threat at the start of each encounter and, after each round of character turns, moves to the character who inflicted the most damage on enemy models during that round (on a tie, or if no damage was inflicted, the holder keeps it). If the holder dies it moves to the living character with the highest threat. In boss encounters it moves to any character who damages the boss during their turn (see §7.5, Encounter Turn Order; §16.4)."
- After: "**Aggro token.** The single token that marks the character with aggro. It is placed on the character with the highest threat at the start of each encounter and, after each round of character turns, moves to the character who inflicted the most damage on enemy models during that round (on a tie, or if no damage was inflicted, the holder keeps it). If the holder dies it moves to the living character with the highest threat. In boss encounters it moves to any character who damages the boss, or another enemy model in the encounter, during their turn (see §7.5, Encounter Turn Order; §16.4)."
- Rationale: [MECHANICAL CHANGE] Keeps the glossary in step with boss-E06.
- Source: final rulings, boss-E35 (`06_final_rulings.md`); follows boss-E06

### CH-21-039 · Glossary, Mini-boss
- Type: completion
- Before: "**Mini-boss.** The first boss of the standard game, fought on the mini-boss tile (see §6.5; §15.3)."
- After: "**Mini-boss.** The first boss of the standard game, fought on the mini-boss tile or, for the Asylum Demon, on the mega-boss game board of its expansion (see §6.5; §15.3; §17.3)."
- Rationale: Keeps the glossary in step with boss-E03 and boss-E11.
- Source: final rulings, boss-E36 (`06_final_rulings.md`); Asylum Demon booklet lines 183-190

### CH-21-040 · Glossary, Beam card (new entry)
- Type: completion
- Before: "**Behaviour card.** A card drawn from the behaviour deck of a boss, mimic, invader, or summon at the start of that model's turn. Its icons are resolved from left to right. A boss's current behaviour card sets the range and the dodge difficulty of its attacks and shows its weak arcs until the next card is drawn (see §15.5; §16.1)."
- After: "**Beam card.** A card from the Old Iron King expansion revealed when a Beam icon is resolved. It shows the Iron King node on which the Old Iron King's model is placed and the nodes whose characters the Beam attack targets (see §17.10, Old Iron King).

**Behaviour card.** A card drawn from the behaviour deck of a boss, mimic, invader, or summon at the start of that model's turn. Its icons are resolved from left to right. A boss's current behaviour card sets the range and the dodge difficulty of its attacks and shows its weak arcs until the next card is drawn (see §15.5; §16.1)."
- Rationale: New defined term of boss-E25.
- Source: final rulings, boss-E38 (`06_final_rulings.md`); Old Iron King booklet lines 435-460

### CH-21-041 · Glossary, Cage node (new entry)
- Type: completion
- Before: "**Calamity.** A condition from the Black Dragon Kalameet expansion. A model holds at most one calamity token, which reduces the total of each of its block, resist, and dodge rolls by one. A calamity token is removed only when the encounter ends (see §8.5, Calamity)."
- After: "**Cage node.** One of the four nodes at the corners of the mega-boss side of the Guardian Dragon's *mega-boss game board*. No model may *move*, *dodge*, or be pushed into a cage node; the Guardian Dragon's model is placed on one only when it resolves an Inferno icon (see §17.10, Guardian Dragon).

**Calamity.** A condition from the Black Dragon Kalameet expansion. A model holds at most one calamity token, which reduces the total of each of its block, resist, and dodge rolls by one. A calamity token is removed only when the encounter ends (see §8.5, Calamity)."
- Rationale: New defined term of boss-E22.
- Source: final rulings, boss-E39 (`06_final_rulings.md`); Guardian Dragon booklet lines 438-444

### CH-21-042 · Glossary, Fiery Breath card (new entry)
- Type: completion
- Before: "**Final boss.** The last boss of the game: the main-boss, or the mega-boss if the players chose to add one. Defeating the final boss wins the game (see §7.1). In campaign play the final boss is the last boss listed in the campaign's last section (see §18.1)."
- After: "**Fiery Breath card.** A card from the Guardian Dragon expansion revealed when an Inferno icon is resolved. It shows the cage node on which the Guardian Dragon's model is placed, the nodes whose characters the Inferno attack targets, and the attack value and dodge difficulty of that attack (see §17.10, Guardian Dragon).

**Final boss.** The last boss of the game: the main-boss, or the mega-boss if the players chose to add one. Defeating the final boss wins the game (see §7.1). In campaign play the final boss is the last boss listed in the campaign's last section (see §18.1)."
- Rationale: New defined term of boss-E22.
- Source: final rulings, boss-E40 (`06_final_rulings.md`); Guardian Dragon booklet lines 446-470 and p. 13 card diagram

### CH-21-043 · Glossary, Iron King node (new entry)
- Type: completion
- Before: "**Leap.** A boss movement marked with the leap icon. The boss is removed from its node and placed on the node of its target, at any distance, without changing its facing. Every character on that node is pushed to a valid adjacent node chosen by that character's player, and the push is not limited by arcs (see §16.2)."
- After: "**Iron King node.** One of the three nodes, printed on the mega-boss side of the Old Iron King's *mega-boss game board*, on which the Old Iron King's model is placed. The Old Iron King never stands on any other node (see §17.10, Old Iron King).

**Leap.** A boss movement marked with the leap icon. The boss is removed from its node and placed on the node of its target, at any distance, without changing its facing. Every character on that node is pushed to a valid adjacent node chosen by that character's player, and the push is not limited by arcs (see §16.2)."
- Rationale: New defined term of boss-E25.
- Source: final rulings, boss-E41 (`06_final_rulings.md`); Old Iron King booklet lines 424-433

### CH-21-044 · Glossary, Stagger
- Type: correction
- Before: "**Stagger.** A condition. A staggered character subtracts one from the total of each attack, block, resist, and dodge roll; any other staggered model reduces its attack values, its block and resist values, and its dodge difficulty by one. The token is removed at the end of the model's next complete turn. Stagger is placed by attacks that bear the stagger icon, by stamina overflow, and by a parry (see §8.5, Stagger; §9.6; §10.1)."
- After: "**Stagger.** A condition. A staggered character or *summon* subtracts one from the total of each attack, block, resist, and dodge roll; any other staggered model reduces its attack values, its block and resist values, and its dodge difficulty by one. The token is removed at the end of the model's next complete turn. Stagger is placed by attacks that bear the stagger icon, by stamina overflow, and by a parry (see §8.5, Stagger; §9.6; §10.1)."
- Rationale: [MECHANICAL CHANGE] Glossary follows §8.5 (lead-E20).
- Source: final rulings, lead-E21 (`06_final_rulings.md`)

### CH-21-045 · Glossary, Round
- Type: correction
- Before: "**Round.** One pass of the encounter turn order: every enemy model takes a turn in descending threat order, then every character takes a turn in descending threat order (see §7.5, Encounter Turn Order)."
- After: "**Round.** One pass of the encounter turn order: every enemy model takes a turn in descending threat order, then every character takes a turn in descending threat order (see §7.5, Encounter Turn Order). In a boss encounter a round is one turn by each living character, in an order the players choose, with one boss turn before each of those turns (see §16.1). In a *PvP encounter* a round is one turn by each character still in play (see §20.2)."
- Rationale: The term is used in italics for the rounds of boss encounters (§16.1) and PvP encounters (§20.2, §20.8), which the entry did not cover.
- Source: final rulings, VB-017 (`06_final_rulings.md`); independent verification, work/phase3/verify_b.md

### CH-21-046 · Glossary, PvP encounter
- Type: correction
- Before: "after each boss's defeat and before the rest that follows it."
- After: "after each boss's defeat and before the rest that follows it (in campaign play, only after a boss whose defeat completes a *section*; see §18.1)."
- Rationale: Carries today's campaign rule of §18.1 into the entry.
- Source: final rulings, VB-018 (`06_final_rulings.md`); independent verification, work/phase3/verify_b.md

### CH-21-047 · Glossary, Shop
- Type: correction
- Before: "The tier one shop is stocked at setup and the tier two shop when the mini-boss is defeated; both are restocked only after a boss is defeated (in campaign play, after a section is completed), unsold cards going to the bottom of their deck first (see §6.3)."
- After: "The tier one shop is stocked at setup and the tier two shop when the mini-boss is defeated (in campaign play, when the *bonfire ascetic* is burnt); both are restocked only after a boss is defeated (in campaign play, after a section is completed), unsold cards going to the bottom of their deck first (see §6.3; §18.6)."
- Rationale: In campaign play the tier two shop opens with the bonfire ascetic, not with a mini-boss (§18.6, §18.7).
- Source: final rulings, VB-019 (`06_final_rulings.md`); independent verification, work/phase3/verify_b.md

### CH-21-048 · Glossary, Node
- Type: correction
- Before: "**Node.** A circular space on a tile. Every model occupies exactly one node. Nodes are basic, spawn or terrain."
- After: "**Node.** A circular space on a tile. During an encounter every model occupies exactly one node. Each node is one of three types: basic, spawn, or terrain (see §5.1)."
- Rationale: The entry cited no section; §5.1 states the rule.
- Source: final rulings, VB-020 (`06_final_rulings.md`); independent verification, work/phase3/verify_b.md

### CH-21-049 · Glossary, Spawn node
- Type: correction
- Before: "**Spawn node.** A node on which enemy models are placed at the start of an encounter. Boss tiles have a boss spawn node."
- After: "**Spawn node.** A node on which enemy models are placed at the start of an encounter, as the encounter card directs. The mini-boss tile, the main-boss tile, and each mega-boss game board have a boss spawn node (see §5.1; §15.3)."
- Rationale: The entry cited no section and omitted the mega-boss spawn node of a game board.
- Source: final rulings, VB-021 (`06_final_rulings.md`); independent verification, work/phase3/verify_b.md

### CH-21-050 · Glossary, Stamina cube
- Type: correction
- Before: "**Stamina cube.** A black cube. Each point of stamina a character spends places one stamina cube on the endurance bar, filling from the left."
- After: "**Stamina cube.** A black cube. Each point of stamina a character spends places one stamina cube on the endurance bar, filling from the left (see §3.4)."
- Rationale: The entry cited no section; §3.4 states the rule.
- Source: final rulings, VB-022 (`06_final_rulings.md`); independent verification, work/phase3/verify_b.md

### CH-21-051 · Glossary, Transposed treasure
- Type: correction
- Before: "**Transposed treasure.** A class treasure card marked with a blue light. All transposed treasure is tier two."
- After: "**Transposed treasure.** A *class-specific treasure* card marked with a blue light in the top-right of the card. All transposed treasure is tier two (see §6.2)."
- Rationale: The entry cited no section and named the parent type otherwise than its headword.
- Source: final rulings, VB-023 (`06_final_rulings.md`); independent verification, work/phase3/verify_b.md

### CH-21-052 · Glossary, Tier one treasure
- Type: correction
- Before: "Encounter rewards and chests draw from tier one until the mini-boss is defeated (in campaign play, until the *bonfire ascetic* is burnt) (see §6.2)."
- After: "Encounter rewards and chests draw from tier one until the mini-boss is defeated (in campaign play, until the *bonfire ascetic* is burnt) (see §6.2; §18.5)."
- Rationale: The campaign clause is stated in §18.5, which was not cited.
- Source: final rulings, VB-024 (`06_final_rulings.md`); independent verification, work/phase3/verify_b.md

### CH-21-053 · Glossary, Tier two treasure
- Type: correction
- Before: "Encounter rewards and chests draw from tier two once the mini-boss is defeated (in campaign play, once the *bonfire ascetic* is burnt) (see §6.2)."
- After: "Encounter rewards and chests draw from tier two once the mini-boss is defeated (in campaign play, once the *bonfire ascetic* is burnt) (see §6.2; §18.5)."
- Rationale: The campaign clause is stated in §18.5, which was not cited.
- Source: final rulings, VB-025 (`06_final_rulings.md`); independent verification, work/phase3/verify_b.md

### CH-21-054 · §21, Attack option
- Type: correction
- Before: "**Attack option.** One line of a weapon or spell card, showing a stamina cost, dice and modifiers. Using an attack option is the *Attack* major action."
- After: "**Attack option.** One line of a weapon or spell card, showing a stamina cost, the dice to roll, any fixed modifier, and any attack icons (see §4.1, Card Anatomy). A character uses one attack option for each attack of the *Attack* major action and for a *riposte* (see §10.3; §9.6)."
- Rationale: By §10.3 the Attack major action may use an attack option of each weapon slot, and by §9.6 a riposte uses an attack option and is not the Attack major action.
- Source: final rulings, VA-028 (`06_final_rulings.md`); second verification, chapters 1 to 11 (work/phase3/verify_a.md)

### CH-21-055 · §21, Common treasure
- Type: correction
- Before: "**Common treasure.** A treasure card that is not starting, class-specific, transposed, legendary or boss treasure."
- After: "**Common treasure.** A treasure card that is not starting equipment, class-specific, transposed, legendary, or boss treasure, and is not an *ember card*, an *invader*'s treasure card, or a Titanite Scale card."
- Rationale: Companion of VA-011: the definition must not sweep cards that §11.4, §12.2 and §14 keep outside the treasure decks into common treasure.
- Source: final rulings, VA-029 (`06_final_rulings.md`); second verification, chapters 1 to 11 (work/phase3/verify_a.md)

### CH-21-056 · §21, Encounter level
- Type: correction
- Before: "and increase from the tile adjacent to the bonfire tile towards the boss tile (see §6.5)."
- After: "and increase along the route from the first encounter tile to the last (see §6.5)."
- Rationale: As VA-014: with a route of any shape two encounter tiles may lie beside the bonfire tile.
- Source: final rulings, VA-030 (`06_final_rulings.md`); second verification, chapters 1 to 11 (work/phase3/verify_a.md)

### CH-21-057 · §21, Enemy scaling
- Type: correction
- Before: "The adjustment of every enemy to the number of characters fixed at setup:"
- After: "The adjustment of every enemy to the number of characters taking part in the game, which changes only when a player joins or leaves (see §18.4):"
- Rationale: §8.3 as now written lets the number change when a player joins or leaves a game in progress (§18.4); it is not fixed at setup.
- Source: final rulings, VA-031 (`06_final_rulings.md`); second verification, chapters 1 to 11 (work/phase3/verify_a.md)

### CH-21-058 · §21, Main-boss
- Type: correction
- Before: "Its defeat wins the standard game unless a mega-boss is in play (see §6.6; §15.3)."
- After: "Its defeat wins the standard game unless a mega-boss is in play (see §6.6; §7.1; §15.3)."
- Rationale: The victory is stated in §7.1 (and §6.7), not in §6.6 or §15.3.
- Source: final rulings, VA-032 (`06_final_rulings.md`); second verification, chapters 1 to 11 (work/phase3/verify_a.md)

### CH-21-059 · §21, Boss spawn node
- Type: correction
- Before: "The boss model is placed on it at the start of every boss encounter (see §15.3)."
- After: "The boss model is placed on it at the start of every boss encounter, unless the boss's special rules name another node: the Asylum Demon, a mini-boss, is placed on the mega-boss spawn node of its expansion's game board (see §15.3; §17.3)."
- Rationale: §15.3 now carries the exception for special rules, and §17.3 places the Asylum Demon mini-boss on a mega-boss spawn node.
- Source: final rulings, VA-036 (`06_final_rulings.md`); second verification, chapters 1 to 11 (work/phase3/verify_a.md)

### CH-21-060 · §21, Boss tile
- Type: correction
- Before: "A mini-boss encounter is fought on the mini-boss tile alone; a main-boss encounter is fought on both boss tiles joined as one tile, the main-boss tile."
- After: "A mini-boss encounter is fought on the mini-boss tile alone, unless the mini-boss's special rules name another tile, as for the Asylum Demon (see §17.3); a main-boss encounter is fought on both boss tiles joined as one tile, the main-boss tile."
- Rationale: §15.3 and §17.3 fight the Asylum Demon mini-boss on its expansion's game board, not on the mini-boss tile.
- Source: final rulings, VA-037 (`06_final_rulings.md`); second verification, chapters 1 to 11 (work/phase3/verify_a.md)

### CH-21-061 · §21, Invasion
- Type: correction
- Before: "no more than one is provoked before the mini-boss is defeated and no more than one after it (see §12.2)."
- After: "no more than one is provoked before the mini-boss is defeated and no more than one after it; in campaign play, no more than one is provoked in each *section* (see §12.2; §18.1)."
- Rationale: §12.2 and §18.1 set the limit in campaign play at one invasion in each section; the entry gave only the standard game's limit.
- Source: final rulings, VA-038 (`06_final_rulings.md`); second verification, chapters 1 to 11 (work/phase3/verify_a.md)

### CH-21-062 · §21, Mimic
- Type: correction
- Before: "the Hungry Mimic (before the mini-boss is defeated) or the Voracious Mimic (afterwards) is fought in a *mimic encounter*."
- After: "the Hungry Mimic (before the mini-boss is defeated; in campaign play, while the current tier is tier one) or the Voracious Mimic (afterwards) is fought in a *mimic encounter*."
- Rationale: §13.2 chooses the mimic in campaign play by the current tier, not by the mini-boss's defeat.
- Source: final rulings, VA-039 (`06_final_rulings.md`); second verification, chapters 1 to 11 (work/phase3/verify_a.md)

### CH-21-063 · §21, Spark (glossary N to Z, outside verifier A's half; companion of VA-001)
- Type: correction
- Before: "and spends one each time the characters rest at the *bonfire* (see §7.4)."
- After: "and spends one each time the characters rest at the *bonfire*, except at the rest that follows the defeat of a boss (see §7.4)."
- Rationale: §7.4 and §11.2 make the rest that follows a boss's defeat free of any spark.
- Source: final rulings, VA-040 (`06_final_rulings.md`); second verification, chapters 1 to 11 (work/phase3/verify_a.md)

### CH-21-064 · §21, Threat (glossary N to Z, outside verifier A's half; companion of VA-004)
- Type: correction
- Before: "the players settle ties (see §3.1; §7.5, Encounter Turn Order)."
- After: "the players settle ties (see §3.1; §7.5, Encounter Start; §7.5, Encounter Turn Order)."
- Rationale: The placing of the aggro token and the settling of ties are stated in §7.5, Encounter Start, step 10.
- Source: final rulings, VA-041 (`06_final_rulings.md`); second verification, chapters 1 to 11 (work/phase3/verify_a.md)

### CH-21-065 · §21, Relevant stat (glossary N to Z, outside verifier A's half; companion of VA-005)
- Type: correction
- Before: "A weapon is *scaled* by comparing the character's relevant stat with that requirement (see §10.5)."
- After: "A weapon is *scaled* by comparing the character's relevant stat with that requirement; a weapon whose stat requirements are all zero has no relevant stat (see §10.5)."
- Rationale: §10.5 gives a weapon whose four stat requirements are all zero no relevant stat, where the entry would let the character choose among four tied stats.
- Source: final rulings, VA-042 (`06_final_rulings.md`); second verification, chapters 1 to 11 (work/phase3/verify_a.md)

### CH-21-066 · §21, Frostbite
- Type: ruling
- Before: "any other model with a frostbite token reduces each of its movement values by one."
- After: "any other model with a frostbite token reduces each of its movement values, or for a *summon* each of its *shift* values, by one."
- Rationale: [MECHANICAL CHANGE] Glossary companion of the frostbite rule for a summon.
- Source: final rulings, VA-024b (`06_final_rulings.md`); lead editor's decision on a judgement call of the second verification (work/phase3/verify_a.md)
